Replaced the final eight literal-address operands in game code with actual native owners: amt_ExecuteOfficeAssignment's two byte reads of office template f8 and one f10 read (three fixups); plt_TableLookup's two plant-pair fields (two); gm_AllocBuildingDefaults' 37-record object-id bound (one); gm_KillSpieler's low-word current year read (one); and gm_AllocGebaeude's remaining object-counter read (one). Fixed two related Rebrew defects directly in sibling rebrew/src/rebrew/matcher/scoring.py. An unresolved inline jump table decodes to different instruction boundaries, causing diff to report structural differences and a missing tail despite a complete relocation-normalized byte match. Such complete matches now share reference boundaries while retaining raw candidate bytes and showing undecodable ranges as data. Explicit full byte matches also bypass the GA/flag-sweep mnemonic penalty; an 11-byte reproducer previously scored 14285.71 despite zero non-relocation differences. A focused regression checks the complete match, invalid relocations and a real unmasked change. Matching/scoring/diff/GA tests: 321 passed; Ruff check/format and mypy pass. Actual building-function diff now exits zero with no structural rows. These changes remain uncommitted alongside prior sibling fixes. Patch backup .scratch/rebrew-inline-pointer-diff.patch.

## Update — 2026-09-30: command table relocations

makes VC6 emit COMMON rather than an early per-object `.bss` definition.

imports remain exact, fixups remain 325 missing / 92 extra. The file's
remaining gap is about 41,583 bytes, including the 28,672-byte size shortfall.
The edited file's four functions retain RELOC in `rebrew test`.

A second control removing `g_log_file_handle = 0` was rejected and reverted:
it moves the crash flag to its exact reference address and reduces `.text`
differences to 4,380, but shifts CRT BSS by eight bytes and introduces 13
wrong `.data` pointer bytes (mlock table and lconv null pointers). Do not
compensate with padding. The error group's remaining storage and original
COMMON ordering must be reconstructed together. Explicit zero definitions
in these VC6 objects reside in `.bss`, not initialized `.data`.

New relocation-size finding: the reference contains **7,404 bytes of actual
fixup blocks and 59,016 zero bytes after them** inside `.reloc` VS 66,420.
The raw link contains **6,912 block bytes and 32,664 zero bytes** inside VS
39,576. The reference directory size includes the reservation (66,420);
the raw directory describes only its blocks (6,912). The old explanation
that 28,672 missing file bytes follow from 494 missing fixups is false.
In native VC6 controls, adding a discarded selectany table of 1,000 pointers
under `/OPT:REF` grows `.reloc` VS from 12 to 2,032 with the live fixup blocks
unchanged at 12 bytes. Controls: `.scratch/reloc_slack_{0,1000}.c` and `.dll`.
This proves original discarded input composition matters to file identity;
it does not yet explain the reference's larger directory-size field.
Rebrew now reports block/directory/section sizes alongside missing fixups.

The zlib empty-string target `0x10034620` coincides with the crash flag in
the raw link, but the reference flag is `0x1003461c`. Do not alias them: the
flag is mutable, whereas zlib needs an empty string. The surrounding raw
error globals are misplaced too (`g_log_mode_flags` at 0x1003462c instead
of 0x10034618, owner window at 0x10034624 instead of 0x117660e0). Native
global ownership/order must be corrected rather than exchanging literals
for pointers to unrelated mutable storage.

Locale replacement is now integrated: `locale_time_strings.c` supplies 42
native const arrays (292 bytes) and `locale_time_table.c` supplies the
43-pointer struct (172 bytes, native ALIGN_8). CMake compiles both; the split
build places their unchanged objects in `.rdata`/`.data` order and excludes
the copied locale string region and old absolute definitions. All strings,
all table pointers, raw `.data`, and import records match the reference.
The raw link now has **325 missing / 92 extra actual base fixups**, with
only **four missing in `.data`** (the two zlib empty-string and two `_iob`
buffer pointers below). Whole-file gap: **12,914 differing + 28,672 missing
bytes**. New locale sources lint cleanly; whole-tree lint has zero errors.
This supersedes the provisional controls and counts below.

Latest continuation: rebrew's whole-binary JSON now reports base fixups by
RVA/type and ignores type-zero ABSOLUTE alignment entries. Current actual
fixups: **368 missing / 92 extra**, including **47 missing in `.data`**.
The older relocation script's 378/96 counts below include padding records.
Upstream targeted checks pass (142 tests).

Native compiler controls in `.scratch/locale_time*.c` established that a
43-element pointer table initialized with the stock locale strings under
`/O1 /Gd /GF` emits all 42 distinct literals in reference order. A plain
array emits ALIGN_4; wrapping that array in a struct naturally emits the
stock ALIGN_8, with the same 172-byte table and no padding declaration.
`__declspec(align(8))` is unsupported by this VC6 compiler. The next build
change can use native source declarations, with strings and table in
separate translation units so `.rdata` and `.data` can be ordered separately.
No locale source replacement is integrated yet.

The two 106-entry arrays in `alchemistry_logic3.c` now initialize named
handlers instead of numeric image addresses. CL emits 212 `.data` relocations;
the raw split link has **378 missing / 96 extra** records, with only **48**
missing in `.data` (previously 260). All 212 linked pointers, the entire raw
`.data` section and full import records match the reference. `rebrew test`
keeps `ahm_ProcessEntityChain` RELOC and `gm_GetFieldByType` EXACT.

The remaining `.data` records are 43 locale pointers at `0x10034260` through
`0x10034308`, two zlib empty-string pointers (`0x100294dc`, `0x100294f8`),
two `_iob` buffer pointers (`0x100317f8`, `0x10031800`), and a type-zero
alignment record. The locale table comes from `strftime.obj`, but
`scripts/emit_crt_data.py` resolves its strings as absolute symbols, so LINK
omits base relocations. The strings must acquire real section ownership.

Rebrew's `verify --whole-binary` now also checks the full raw file bytes;
structural parity alone cannot pass. `scripts/verify_objective.py` defaults
to the raw link and requires equality. The current raw-link gap is **12,934
differing bytes + 28,672 missing bytes**. These updates supersede the old
counts below; the objective remains unmet.

This is the state to read first. `docs/TODO.md` is the full log (newest note first);
this file is the short version and the list of things that are easy to waste a round on.

## Verified state

Measured by `bash scripts/gate.sh` immediately before this file was written. The gate
prints 25 rows; **24 pass and one fails**, and the failing one is true.

| Quantity | Value |
|---|---|
| deliverable `build/server.dll` | **byte-identical to the reference** (286,720 bytes, whole-file `cmp`) |
| `rebrew verify --full` | **262 / 262** |
| `lint` | **0 errors, 0 warnings** |
| raw-link `.text` residue | **0** (baseline 4) |
| raw-link `.data` divergence | **0** (baseline 0) |
| relocation records | **590 missing / 96 extra** (baseline held) |
| raw-link import table | **matches the reference (100)** |
| raw-link load | **OK** |
| **OBJECTIVE GAP** (raw link vs reference) | **41,565 bytes** = 12,893 differing + 28,672 size shortfall |
| **postlink layout-map coverage** | **5,143 / 7,463 (68.9%)** — floor is 90% |
| **`cmake --build`** | **exit 2** — the one red row |

`build/server.dll` is byte-identical because `rebrew build postlink` builds it from the
reference. **That is not the objective.** The objective is the raw link
(`build/split_poc.dll`) being identical straight out of the toolchain, and it is 41,565
bytes away.

## The objective, clause by clause

**Clause 1 — "100% file identical straight out of the toolchain, without post linking
mangling" — NOT MET, and the evidence says it is an input problem.** Seven families of
lever were measured; the summary table is in `docs/TODO.md` round 253. The residue is:
a CRT archive whose import references differ, an `WS2_32` import library the reference
linked by ordinal, and `link.exe`'s own ordering decisions. Round 225 is the control:
one of those "linker-internal" conclusions was wrong and inverting a mapping fixed the
import table exactly, so the others were re-tested and held.

**Clause 2 — "fix all tooling deficits ... fix them directly in rebrew" — served, and
open by construction.** Seven gate checks were added or repaired and six rebrew fixes
were made this session. Details below.

## Do not re-derive these — they are measured

- **`rebrew status`'s denominator is `|annotated ∪ (inventory − library)|`**
  (`status.py:437`). *library* = VAs with a `// LIBRARY:` marker or a module in
  `external_libs`. It reads **262/335**. Round 248 explains why it moved from 280: the
  inventory regeneration recovered 101 functions that merged entries had hidden, 44 are
  excluded as library and ~55 are **unattributed** — some are game code, some are CRT.
  Attributing them needs a body to compare against, so it is manual work.
- **`g_dispatch_table` is `size = 4` on purpose.** The rest of its 212 pointers are
  covered by placeholder entries (`g_before_g_chain_handler_table` and its neighbours in
  `src/crt_region/section_spans.c`). Setting `size = 848` makes the data-extents check
  fail, correctly. Round 257.
- **`/TIMESTAMP` does not exist in LINK 6.0** (`LNK4044`). The two clock bytes cannot be
  controlled at link time. Round 241.
- **The link is deterministic except for two bytes** (the PE `TimeDateStamp` and its copy
  at `.rdata+0x2e34`). A gap reading that differs across runs is not link content.
  Rounds 239-240.
- **The raw link's import table matches now** — that was fixed in round 225 by inverting
  the `/include` ordering mapping in `src/crt_region/crt_imports.c`. Do not "fix" it.
- **`build/abs__z_errmsg_empty.obj` is deliberate**, not debris. Round 243.

## Operational hazards (each has cost a round)

1. **The `rebrew` checkout gets moved off `main` by something outside this project.**
   It happened three times. When it does, `scripts/postlink_residual.py` dies with
   `ImportError` and six gate rows fail for one missing symbol. The gate now prints
   `rebrew HEAD:` and checks four symbols every run. Recovery is two commands:
   ```sh
   cd /home/maci/Desktop/Projects/relumea/rebrew
   git checkout main
   git apply /home/maci/Desktop/Projects/relumea/guild-rebrew/.scratch/rebrew-session-fixes.patch
   ```
   `.scratch/` is gitignored, so a clean loses the patch — the same fixes are listed by
   symbol in `docs/TODO.md` round 252 to be rewritten if needed.
2. **`cmake --build build` fails at postlink by design** (coverage below the floor). The
   gate's own build step is what measures it; do not read a non-zero exit as a broken tree.
3. **`src/rebrew-data.toml` is mode `-r--r--r--`.** `chmod u+w`, edit, `chmod u-w`.
4. **Never `git checkout --` a directory in this tree**; uncommitted work has been
   destroyed that way three times in project history.
5. **The docs' CURRENT STATE table is generated.** `scripts/gate.sh` calls
   `scripts/update_state_table.py` at the end of every run. Do not hand-edit between the
   markers.

## The remaining work, scoped

**a) The `.data` fixups — 260 of the reference's 334 relocation records are absent.**
Round 255 grouped them: **47 contiguous tables**, the largest **212 entries** at
`.data 0x02c3f8..0x02c744`, then 43 entries at 0x034260, two of 10, and 45 of ten or
fewer. The fix is to declare each table in `src/crt_region/section_spans.c` as an array
of the right pointer type initialised with the symbols its entries name, so the compiler
emits the pointers and the linker records the relocations. Offsets, values and names are
all known — the targets are image addresses the locator resolves. **This is the concrete
expression of "without post linking mangling": the bytes are right today, but they arrive
from a blob rather than from declarations.**

**b) The last third of `.text`.** Round 215: the two instruction streams agree for
**36,575 instructions unbroken**, then diverge at `.text+0x19160` and only sporadically
realign — 25% of the section. Round 236 decomposed postlink's failing call entries:
**882 of 1,117 are not a call at all in our build**. That is code we have not reproduced,
and it is what the 90% coverage floor is measuring.

**c) 55 unattributed recovered functions** (see the `rebrew status` note above).

## Rebrew changes made this session

Saved as `.scratch/rebrew-session-fixes.patch` (420 lines, 2 files, verified to apply to
a clean tree). All are in `discover.py` and `verify.py`; nothing was committed to that
checkout.

| symbol | purpose | rounds |
|---|---|---|
| `discover._starts_function` | eight prologue shapes = "a function ends here" | 193, 196-197, 245 |
| (discover sweep change) | record the first `ret` **and** the first `ret` a prologue follows | 195 |
| (discover recovery) | insert the missed function as a candidate before sizing | 194 |
| `discover._prologue_read_failed` | one warning per run when a read fails, instead of silence | 219-220 |
| `verify._looks_like_inventory_merge` | merge vs truncated annotation, from the bytes | 192, 247 |
| `verify._pe_sections_for_merge_check` | minimal section reader for the check above | 192 |
| (verify print) | the size-divergence warning in the human output | 189 |

None of these moved the OBJECTIVE GAP. Their effect was on measurement: **all twelve size
divergences turned out to be inventory defects, not truncated annotations**, and the list
went from 12 entries / 3,771 bytes to 1 entry / 17 bytes.

| check | state before | after |
|---|---|---|
| whole-file comparison | did not exist | deliverable asserted byte-identical (166) |
| relocation records | not measured | `scripts/reloc_set_check.py`, baseline 590/96 (170) |
| build exit status | string match only, could not fail | real exit status, postlink named (183) |
| raw-link import table | compared the reference with its own copy | real comparison, **found 15 differing entries** (210) |
| coverage ratio | buried in an error blob | a named row (234) |
| rebrew checkout | unchecked | HEAD + symbols + recovery patch (249-250) |
| CURRENT STATE table | hand-maintained, stale twice | generated (238) |

**The lesson worth keeping: four of these could not fail, and all four looked green.**
The countermeasure that worked every time was reading the check rather than its output.
